West of House
You are standing in an open field west of a white house, with a boarded front door.
There is a small mailbox here.


Starting location: West House
Target score: 80
Model: openai/gpt-4o-mini | Agent type: smart
Maximum steps: 500

LESSONS LEARNED FROM PREVIOUS EPISODES:
Repeat these positive behaviors:
1. Climb and examine trees, mailbox and other local features early; here, taking the egg in the tree was the only confirmed +5.
2. After a score, retrace the exact inbound path (Up a Tree → Forest Path → North House → West House) instead of improvising a route.
3. Check each exit once per room and keep a written list of tried directions; prefer untried exits over repeating known loops.
4. Verify any score claim against a recorded score delta before repeating an action; take egg gave +5 once, and re-takes gave 0.
5. Use plain verbs (open, take, examine) on listed objects; they gave clear feedback, while odd compound commands gave jokes or damage.
Avoid these negative behaviors:
1. Do not throw, shake or otherwise roughly handle fragile treasures; 'throw egg at tree' damaged the egg and shaking repeatedly spilled its contents.
2. Do not repeat the open/close/shake/put cycle on the same container more than once; about 30 such steps gave 0 score.
3. Do not loop Forest Path, Clearing and Forest with 'go around' commands; about 60 moves there found no new route or score.
4. Do not use ordinary items as tools on a treasure (leaflet in egg, canary in egg) without a clue; each attempt gave 0 and no new information.
5. Do not keep calling a return-route goal while moving randomly; with no plan back to the House, reread the observation and room descriptions for exit hints.
Apply these lessons when relevant; current game feedback takes precedence.

Actor RL selection method: model_label_logits_plus_advantage_nsi
Observation: Up a Tree
You are about 10 feet above the ground nestled among some large branches. The nearest branch above you is above your reach.
Beside you on the branch is a small bird's nest.
In the bird's nest is a large egg encrusted with precious jewels, apparently scavenged by a childless songbird. The egg is covered with fine gold inlay, and ornamented in lapis lazuli and mother-of-pearl. Unlike most eggs, this one is hinged and closed with a delicate looking clasp. The egg appears extremely fragile.

Observation: There is a noticeable crunch from beneath you, and inspection reveals that the egg is lying open, badly damaged. There is a golden clockwork canary nestled in the egg. It seems to have recently had a bad experience. The mountings for its jewel-like eyes are empty, and its silver beak is crumpled. Through a cracked crystal window below its left wing you can see the remains of intricate machinery. It is not clear what result winding it would have, as the mainspring seems sprung.
You hear in the distance the chirping of a song bird.

Observation: Canyon View
You are at the top of the Great Canyon on its west wall. From here there is a marvelous view of the canyon and parts of the Frigid River upstream. Across the canyon, the walls of the White Cliffs join the mighty ramparts of the Flathead Mountains to the east. Following the Canyon upstream to the north, Aragain Falls may be seen, complete with rainbow. The mighty Frigid River flows out from a great dark cavern. To the west and south can be seen an immense forest, stretching for miles around. A path leads northwest. It is possible to climb down into the canyon from here.

Historian episode review — episode 3
================================================

Review source: llm
Lessons: 5 positive + 5 negative (configured per category: 5)

The Actor's only confirmed score was +5 for 'take egg' at Up a Tree, and the evidence shows no other score event. The egg later appears as 'broken jewel-encrusted egg' in goal text, so it was damaged early, but the visible trajectory is truncated and does not show how. Its strongest decision was heading to a known productive spot. After that it stalled. The late trajectory is a loop of take leaves / put down leaves / throw leaves at tree, up/down the tree, and 'go around forest' / Clearing / CanyView shuttles. All of it scored 0 and found nothing new. Return-route goals repeated without a plan, and the Actor never reached the House or underground. Late in the run it twice moved south from Forest Path to North House and went straight back, instead of exploring from there. Next episode, handle the egg gently, stop leaf and tree loops quickly, and carry the treasure along unexplored exits from North House or Behind House. Symbolic navigation did not deviate; the weak play was the Actor's own choices.

LESSONS LEARNED
================================
POSITIVES TO REPEAT
1. Take the egg in the tree once; it was the only confirmed score event (+5). Repeat visits scored 0, so do not return.
2. Use plain verbs (take, open, examine) on listed objects; they gave clear feedback, while odd compound commands only gave jokes or no effect.
3. After about 10 zero-score steps, switch to an unexplored exit; North House and Behind House were visited only briefly.
4. Track tried exits per room and try each untried one before revisiting; CanyView and North House got far less attention than the loops.
5. Before reusing a past scoring action, check that its score delta recurs; take egg gave +5 once and nothing on later attempts.

NEGATIVES TO AVOID
1. Do not repeat take leaves / put down leaves / throw leaves cycles; about 100 such steps gave 0 score and no new information.
2. Do not shuttle Forest Path to Up a Tree and back without a new reason; it was repeated dozens of times for nothing.
